FUNDED TRADES (25; only lots the shared allocator funded — refused shadow signals are in signals.csv; $100,000.00 per trade, never compounded)
  fills: the engine's raw execution prices; friction = 5 bps per side on each fill (commission $0); interest = 11% simple on $100,000.00 for the session-date days held (interestDays in the CSV);
  gross P&L = shares × (exit fill − entry fill) before costs; net = gross − friction − interest (the engine's net);
  MAE = the engine's worst minute mark while held (shares × last price − principal − accrued interest).

▶ randomized equivalence
  ✔ engine = replay under every policy, with accounting, arming and end offsets (361.79824ms)

▶ P1 scenarios
  ✔ a sale due after the last event executes; the replacement is still reserved at the end (0.754716ms)
  ✔ a maximum-hold exit supersedes the switch; the replacement fills minutes later (0.471044ms)
  ✔ a replacement can be preempted from the bar after its purchase (a two-minute chain) (0.528134ms)
  ✔ portfolio mode: margin that binds only with the incumbent open means no preemption (2.430159ms)
  ✔ a closing-print sale executes when the replacement fills at the next session’s open (0.896127ms)
  ✔ an own exit in the decision minute frees the pool: a plain grant, not a preemption (0.744126ms)
  ✔ a friction-driven abort at the same-session next trade (0.507574ms)
  ✔ the $250 buffer: exactly $250 switches, $249.99 does not (0.998798ms)
  ✔ windowed figures equal the engine’s daily marks after the boundary (19.677069ms)
  ✔ a refused signal is never reconsidered: the book is funded only on a later fresh signal (0.565224ms)
  ✔ P0 equals the frozen engine with a fifth, candidate book (2.906782ms)
  ✔ auction prints at the close and at a later session’s opening trade (confirmatory review cases) (1.672833ms)
  ✔ a mutation of every array after the switch leaves the switch unchanged (0.862646ms)
✔ P1 scenarios (33.495974ms)

▶ stop policies
  ✔ net: inactive while a fill at the stop would lose after interest, active once above it, exits at the stop level (1.951985ms)
  ✔ gross breakeven (B-B) latches when the stop level reaches the entry price and holds through a loss-making sale (0.464203ms)
  ✔ gross threshold (B-A) activates at the configured appreciation, not before, and is a latch (0.141942ms)
  ✔ always (B-D) is active from the session after the entry, and a gap through it fills at the open (0.243352ms)
  ✔ two-stage (B-C): the protective stop acts in the EARLY stage, the trailing stop after the breakeven latch (0.287152ms)
  ✔ netLatched never switches off; net can (3.559427ms)
  ✔ replayPosition reproduces the engine exit of a given fill (0.201071ms)
  ✔ the maximum hold still sells a losing position whatever the policy, and the buffer only acts after it applies (0.433574ms)
✔ stop policies (7.91947ms)
▶ entry modes
  ✔ always: arms on the first eligible minute, buys on the rebound from the lowest later low, re-arms after an exit, never on the exit session (0.493943ms)
  ✔ swing: the rolling low excludes the current session, a close at or below it arms, the rebound buys (0.444883ms)
  ✔ C3 arbitration: an armed swing setup yields to the deep path on the minute D1 would arm, and the window stays open (0.351533ms)
  ✔ path causality: nothing arms before the eligibility session, and the rolling low never sees the current session (0.230122ms)
✔ entry modes (1.664552ms)

▶ entry-day activation in a book
  ✔ a same-session adverse move after the fill exits at L that session (next-session waits for the next open) (0.512664ms)
  ✔ a fill-minute low through L exits in the fill minute itself (stop fill) or at the next minute’s open (next-open) (0.263572ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a minute opening through L on the entry day loses more than the 8% distance (0.160421ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a position the entry day did not stop gaps far through L the next morning (0.155092ms)
  ✔ a closing-print fill has nothing to observe: identical to next-session (0.207472ms)
  ✔ an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade and is protected from that trade on (0.199192ms)
  ✔ counts as a failed attempt for the decline gate (K = 1 blocks the next arming) (0.180941ms)
  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to an entry-day fill (0.123211ms)
  ✔ repeated runs are byte-identical; the book key carries the rc2 controls (0.286413ms)
✔ entry-day activation in a book (2.411039ms)

▶ eligible observations after the fill

  ✔ a later minute opening at or below L fills at that open (below L: not a loss cap) (0.143351ms)
  ✔ the first minute of the session (an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade) (0.082301ms)
  ✔ a fill in the last minute: only that minute’s low; under next-open it fills at the closing print (0.175721ms)
  ✔ a delayed fill after missing minutes: observations start at the fill minute, the gap is not read (0.114311ms)
  ✔ a closing-print fill: no observation follows in its session (0.060121ms)
  ✔ is inclusive at L and silent a hair above it (0.102731ms)
✔ eligible observations after the fill (2.59825ms)

▶ the protective stop in a book
  ✔ on the crash path: a gap exit below the stop level, with an exact net (not a loss cap) (0.420093ms)

  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to protective fills (as to trailing-stop fills) (0.232792ms)
  ✔ never opens a continuation window (a protective exit always loses) (0.215622ms)

▶ the decline gate
  ✔ K = 1: blocks arming after a failed attempt, logs each refused session, and ends at the cooldown (0.639495ms)
  ✔ K = 2: the second attempt is "within allowance", the second failure blocks, and the block expires after its sessions (0.236082ms)
  ✔ a recovery of r of the decline ends the episode at a session close (0.173351ms)
  ✔ a profitable exit ends an unblocked episode, and the continuation re-entry is untouched (0.248522ms)
  ✔ never blocks a book without failures (the recovery path is unchanged) (0.174762ms)
✔ the decline gate (1.601313ms)
▶ the hold policies in a book

  ✔ refuses the disabled hold without the stop, at the runner boundary (0.293852ms)
  ✔ adaptive: logs the latched extension; the exits are the legacy ones on this path (0.331323ms)
  ✔ adaptive: refuses an extended bound not above the book’s hold (0.105291ms)
  ✔ adaptive without progress exits at the base hold (a shorter base than legacy cuts the failed lot earlier) (0.136071ms)
✔ the hold policies in a book (1.220689ms)

  ✔ protects a position that was never profitable (the legacy stop is inactive) (0.216851ms)
